========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\App.jsx ==========


import { Routes, Route, Navigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Toaster } from "react-hot-toast";

import AdminPage from "./pages/adminPage";
import RegisterPage from "./pages/registerPage";
import LoginPage from "./pages/loginPage";
import ReceptionistPage from "./pages/receptionistPage";
import DoctorPage from "./pages/doctorPage";

function App() {
  return (
    <>
      <Toaster position="top-right" />

      <Routes>
        <Route path="/" element={<Navigate to="/login" replace />} />

        <Route path="/login" element={<LoginPage />} />

        <Route path="/register" element={<RegisterPage />} />

        <Route path="/admin/*" element={<AdminPage />} />

        <Route path="/receptionist/*" element={<ReceptionistPage />} />

        <Route path="/doctor/*" element={<DoctorPage />} />

        <Route path="*" element={<Navigate to="/login" replace />} />
      </Routes>
    </>
  );
}

export default App;

========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminAppointmentFormPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function AdminAppointmentFormPage() {
  const { appointmentId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const isEdit = Boolean(appointmentId);
  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  const headers = { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };

  const [patients, setPatients] = useState([]);
  const [doctors, setDoctors] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(isEdit);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    patientId: "",
    doctorId: "",
    appointmentDate: "",
    reason: "",
    status: "SCHEDULED",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    async function loadData() {
      try {
        const requests = [
          api.get("/patients", { headers }),
          api.get("/doctors", { headers }),
        ];

        if (isEdit) {
          requests.push(
            api.get(`/appointments/${appointmentId}`, { headers })
          );
        }

        const responses = await Promise.all(requests);

        setPatients(responses[0].data.patients || []);
        setDoctors(responses[1].data.doctors || []);

        if (isEdit) {
          const appointment = responses[2].data.appointment;

          setFormData({
            patientId: appointment.patientId,
            doctorId: appointment.doctorId,
            appointmentDate: new Date(appointment.appointmentDate)
              .toISOString()
              .slice(0, 16),
            reason: appointment.reason || "",
            status: appointment.status,
          });
        }
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error(error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load data");
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    loadData();
  }, [appointmentId]);

  function handleChange(e) {
    setFormData({
      ...formData,
      [e.target.name]: e.target.value,
    });
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    try {
      const payload = {
        ...formData,
        appointmentDate: new Date(formData.appointmentDate).toISOString(),
      };

      if (isEdit) {
        await api.put(`/appointments/${appointmentId}`, payload, { headers });
        toast.success("Appointment updated successfully");
      } else {
        await api.post("/appointments", payload, { headers });
        toast.success("Appointment created successfully");
      }

      navigate("/admin/appointments");
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error.response?.data?.message || "Something went wrong");
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return <p className="text-gray-500">Loading...</p>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl">
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
          {isEdit ? "Edit Appointment" : "Add Appointment"}
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
          Enter appointment information
        </p>
      </div>

      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        className="space-y-5 rounded-xl border bg-white p-6"
      >
        <div>
          <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
            Patient
          </label>

          <select
            name="patientId"
            value={formData.patientId}
            onChange={handleChange}
            required
            className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
          >
            <option value="">Select Patient</option>
            {patients.map((patient) => (
              <option key={patient.id} value={patient.id}>
                {patient.firstName} {patient.lastName}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
            Doctor
          </label>

          <select
            name="doctorId"
            value={formData.doctorId}
            onChange={handleChange}
            required
            className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
          >
            <option value="">Select Doctor</option>
            {doctors.map((doctor) => (
              <option key={doctor.id} value={doctor.id}>
                Dr. {doctor.user?.firstName} {doctor.user?.lastName}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
            Appointment Date & Time
          </label>

          <input
            type="datetime-local"
            name="appointmentDate"
            value={formData.appointmentDate}
            onChange={handleChange}
            required
            className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
            Reason
          </label>

          <textarea
            name="reason"
            value={formData.reason}
            onChange={handleChange}
            rows="3"
            className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
            placeholder="Enter appointment reason"
          />
        </div>

        {isEdit && (
          <div>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Status
            </label>

            <select
              name="status"
              value={formData.status}
              onChange={handleChange}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
            >
              <option value="SCHEDULED">Scheduled</option>
              <option value="CONFIRMED">Confirmed</option>
              <option value="COMPLETED">Completed</option>
            </select>
          </div>
        )}

        <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="rounded-lg bg-blue-700 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800"
          >
            {isEdit ? "Update Appointment" : "Create Appointment"}
          </button>

          <Link
            to="/admin/appointments"
            className="rounded-lg border px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50"
          >
            Cancel
          </Link>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminAppointmentsPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Plus, Pencil, XCircle } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function AdminAppointmentsPage() {
  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  const headers = { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };

  async function fetchAppointments() {
    try {
      const response = await api.get("/appointments", { headers });
      setAppointments(response.data.appointments || []);
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load appointments");
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAppointments();
  }, []);

  async function handleCancel(appointmentId) {
    if (!window.confirm("Are you sure you want to cancel this appointment?")) {
      return;
    }

    try {
      await api.patch(
        `/appointments/${appointmentId}/cancel`,
        {},
        { headers }
      );

      toast.success("Appointment cancelled successfully");
      fetchAppointments();
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to cancel appointment");
    }
  }

  function formatDate(date) {
    return new Date(date).toLocaleString();
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
            Appointment Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
            Manage hospital appointments
          </p>
        </div>

        <Link
          to="/admin/appointments/add"
          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800"
        >
          <Plus size={18} />
          Add Appointment
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border bg-white">
        {loading ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">Loading appointments...</p>
        ) : appointments.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">No appointments found.</p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600">
              <tr>
                <th className="p-4">Patient</th>
                <th className="p-4">Doctor</th>
                <th className="p-4">Date & Time</th>
                <th className="p-4">Reason</th>
                <th className="p-4">Status</th>
                <th className="p-4">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {appointments.map((appointment) => (
                <tr key={appointment.id} className="border-t">
                  <td className="p-4">
                    {appointment.patient?.firstName}{" "}
                    {appointment.patient?.lastName}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    Dr. {appointment.doctor?.user?.firstName}{" "}
                    {appointment.doctor?.user?.lastName}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {formatDate(appointment.appointmentDate)}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">{appointment.reason || "-"}</td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700">
                      {appointment.status}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                      {appointment.status !== "CANCELLED" &&
                        appointment.status !== "COMPLETED" && (
                          <>
                            <Link
                              to={`/admin/appointments/edit/${appointment.id}`}
                              className="text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
                              title="Edit"
                            >
                              <Pencil size={18} />
                            </Link>

                            <button
                              onClick={() => handleCancel(appointment.id)}
                              className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800"
                              title="Cancel"
                            >
                              <XCircle size={18} />
                            </button>
                          </>
                        )}

                      {(appointment.status === "CANCELLED" ||
                        appointment.status === "COMPLETED") && (
                        <span className="text-gray-400">No actions</span>
                      )}
                    </div>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminBillingPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Plus, Eye } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function AdminBillingPage() {
  const [invoices, setInvoices] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  const headers = { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchInvoices() {
      try {
        const response = await api.get("/invoices", { headers });
        setInvoices(response.data.invoices || []);
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load invoices"
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchInvoices();
  }, []);

  function formatAmount(amount) {
    return Number(amount || 0).toFixed(2);
  }

  function statusStyle(status) {
    if (status === "PAID") {
      return "bg-green-100 text-green-700";
    }

    if (status === "PARTIALLY_PAID") {
      return "bg-yellow-100 text-yellow-700";
    }

    return "bg-red-100 text-red-700";
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
            Billing Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
            Manage invoices and patient payments
          </p>
        </div>

        <Link
          to="/admin/billing/add"
          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800"
        >
          <Plus size={18} />
          Create Invoice
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border bg-white">
        {loading ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">Loading invoices...</p>
        ) : invoices.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">No invoices found.</p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600">
              <tr>
                <th className="p-4">Patient</th>
                <th className="p-4">Appointment Date</th>
                <th className="p-4">Total Amount</th>
                <th className="p-4">Paid Amount</th>
                <th className="p-4">Status</th>
                <th className="p-4">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {invoices.map((invoice) => (
                <tr key={invoice.id} className="border-t">
                  <td className="p-4">
                    {invoice.patient?.firstName}{" "}
                    {invoice.patient?.lastName}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {invoice.appointment?.appointmentDate
                      ? new Date(
                          invoice.appointment.appointmentDate
                        ).toLocaleDateString()
                      : "-"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {formatAmount(invoice.totalAmount)}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {formatAmount(invoice.paidAmount)}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    <span
                      className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${statusStyle(
                        invoice.status
                      )}`}
                    >
                      {invoice.status?.replaceAll("_", " ")}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    <Link
                      to={`/admin/billing/${invoice.id}`}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
                    >
                      <Eye size={18} />
                      View
                    </Link>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminCreateInvoicePage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function AdminCreateInvoicePage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  const headers = { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };

  const [patients, setPatients] = useState([]);
  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    patientId: "",
    appointmentId: "",
    totalAmount: "",
    paidAmount: "0",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchData() {
      try {
        const [patientsResponse, appointmentsResponse] = await Promise.all([
          api.get("/patients", { headers }),
          api.get("/appointments", { headers }),
        ]);

        setPatients(patientsResponse.data.patients || []);
        setAppointments(appointmentsResponse.data.appointments || []);
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load data"
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchData();
  }, []);

  const availableAppointments = appointments.filter(
    (appointment) =>
      appointment.patientId === formData.patientId &&
      appointment.status !== "CANCELLED"
  );

  function handleChange(e) {
    const { name, value } = e.target;

    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
      ...(name === "patientId" ? { appointmentId: "" } : {}),
    }));
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    const totalAmount = Number(formData.totalAmount);
    const paidAmount = Number(formData.paidAmount);

    if (
      !Number.isFinite(totalAmount) ||
      !Number.isFinite(paidAmount) ||
      totalAmount <= 0 ||
      paidAmount < 0 ||
      paidAmount > totalAmount
    ) {
      toast.error("Please enter valid invoice amounts");
      return;
    }

    try {
      await api.post(
        "/invoices",
        {
          patientId: formData.patientId,
          appointmentId: formData.appointmentId,
          totalAmount,
          paidAmount,
        },
        { headers }
      );

      toast.success("Invoice created successfully");
      navigate("/admin/billing");
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to create invoice"
      );
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return <p className="text-gray-500">Loading...</p>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl">
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
          Create Invoice
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
          Enter invoice and payment information
        </p>
      </div>

      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        className="space-y-5 rounded-xl border bg-white p-6"
      >
        <div>
          <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
            Patient
          </label>

          <select
            name="patientId"
            value={formData.patientId}
            onChange={handleChange}
            required
            className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
          >
            <option value="">Select Patient</option>
            {patients.map((patient) => (
              <option key={patient.id} value={patient.id}>
                {patient.firstName} {patient.lastName}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
            Appointment
          </label>

          <select
            name="appointmentId"
            value={formData.appointmentId}
            onChange={handleChange}
            required
            disabled={!formData.patientId}
            className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600 disabled:bg-gray-100"
          >
            <option value="">Select Appointment</option>

            {availableAppointments.map((appointment) => (
              <option key={appointment.id} value={appointment.id}>
                {new Date(
                  appointment.appointmentDate
                ).toLocaleString()}{" "}
                - {appointment.status}
              </option>
            ))}
          </select>

          {formData.patientId && availableAppointments.length === 0 && (
            <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-500">
              No available appointments for this patient.
            </p>
          )}
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
            Total Amount
          </label>

          <input
            type="number"
            name="totalAmount"
            value={formData.totalAmount}
            onChange={handleChange}
            min="0.01"
            step="0.01"
            required
            className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
            placeholder="Enter total amount"
          />
        </div>

        <div>
          <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
            Initial Paid Amount
          </label>

          <input
            type="number"
            name="paidAmount"
            value={formData.paidAmount}
            onChange={handleChange}
            min="0"
            step="0.01"
            required
            className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
            placeholder="Enter paid amount"
          />
        </div>

        <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="rounded-lg bg-blue-700 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800"
          >
            Create Invoice
          </button>

          <Link
            to="/admin/billing"
            className="rounded-lg border px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50"
          >
            Cancel
          </Link>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminDoctorFormPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

import api from "../lib/api.js";

export default function AdminDoctorFormPage() {
  const { doctorId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const isEdit = Boolean(doctorId);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    firstName: "",
    lastName: "",
    email: "",
    password: "",
    specialization: "",
    licenseNumber: "",
  });

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(isEdit);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isEdit) {
      fetchDoctor();
    }
  }, [doctorId]);

  async function fetchDoctor() {
    try {
      const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

      const response = await api.get(`/doctors/${doctorId}`, {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      });

      const doctor = response.data.doctor;

      setFormData({
        firstName: doctor.user?.firstName || "",
        lastName: doctor.user?.lastName || "",
        email: doctor.user?.email || "",
        password: "",
        specialization: doctor.specialization || "",
        licenseNumber: doctor.licenseNumber || "",
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Fetch doctor error:", error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load doctor"
      );
      navigate("/admin/doctors");
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function handleChange(e) {
    const { name, value } = e.target;

    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (
      !formData.firstName.trim() ||
      !formData.lastName.trim() ||
      !formData.email.trim()
    ) {
      toast.error("First name, last name and email are required");
      return;
    }

    if (!isEdit && formData.password.length < 8) {
      toast.error("Password must contain at least 8 characters");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setIsSubmitting(true);

      const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

      const config = {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      };

      const data = {
        firstName: formData.firstName.trim(),
        lastName: formData.lastName.trim(),
        email: formData.email.trim(),
        specialization: formData.specialization.trim() || null,
        licenseNumber: formData.licenseNumber.trim() || null,
      };

      if (!isEdit) {
        data.password = formData.password;
        await api.post("/doctors", data, config);
        toast.success("Doctor account created successfully");
      } else {
        await api.put(`/doctors/${doctorId}`, data, config);
        toast.success("Doctor details updated successfully");
      }

      navigate("/admin/doctors");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Save doctor error:", error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to save doctor"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <p className="py-10 text-center text-gray-500">
        Loading doctor details...
      </p>
    );
  }

  const inputClass =
    "mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-700";

  return (
    <div>
      <Link
        to="/admin/doctors"
        className="mb-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-blue-900 hover:text-blue-700"
      >
        <ArrowLeft size={18} />
        Back to Doctors
      </Link>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-800">
          {isEdit ? "Edit Doctor" : "Add Doctor"}
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-2 text-gray-500">
          {isEdit
            ? "Update doctor account information"
            : "Create a new doctor account"}
        </p>
      </div>

      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        className="max-w-3xl space-y-6 rounded-xl border bg-white p-6"
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              First Name *
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="firstName"
              value={formData.firstName}
              onChange={handleChange}
              required
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter first name"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Last Name *
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="lastName"
              value={formData.lastName}
              onChange={handleChange}
              required
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter last name"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Email *
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              name="email"
              value={formData.email}
              onChange={handleChange}
              required
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter email address"
            />
          </div>

          {!isEdit && (
            <div>
              <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                Password *
              </label>
              <input
                type="password"
                name="password"
                value={formData.password}
                onChange={handleChange}
                minLength={8}
                required
                className={inputClass}
                placeholder="Minimum 8 characters"
              />
            </div>
          )}

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Specialization
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="specialization"
              value={formData.specialization}
              onChange={handleChange}
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter specialization"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              License Number
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="licenseNumber"
              value={formData.licenseNumber}
              onChange={handleChange}
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter license number"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 border-t pt-5">
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            className="rounded-lg bg-blue-900 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800 disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {isSubmitting
              ? "Saving..."
              : isEdit
                ? "Update Doctor"
                : "Create Doctor"}
          </button>

          <Link
            to="/admin/doctors"
            className="rounded-lg border px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100"
          >
            Cancel
          </Link>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminDoctorsPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Plus, Search, Pencil, UserX } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

import api from "../lib/api.js";

export default function AdminDoctorsPage() {
  const [doctors, setDoctors] = useState([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  async function fetchDoctors() {
    try {
      const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

      const response = await api.get("/doctors", {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      });

      setDoctors(response.data.doctors || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Fetch doctors error:", error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load doctors"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchDoctors();
  }, []);

  async function handleDeactivate(doctorId) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      "Are you sure you want to deactivate this doctor account?"
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    try {
      const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

      await api.delete(`/doctors/${doctorId}`, {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      });

      toast.success("Doctor deactivated successfully");

      setDoctors((previous) =>
        previous.filter((doctor) => doctor.id !== doctorId)
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Deactivate doctor error:", error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to deactivate doctor"
      );
    }
  }

  const filteredDoctors = doctors.filter((doctor) => {
    const fullName =
      `${doctor.user?.firstName || ""} ${doctor.user?.lastName || ""}`.toLowerCase();

    return (
      fullName.includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      doctor.user?.email?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      doctor.specialization?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      doctor.licenseNumber?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase())
    );
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-800">
            Doctor Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-gray-500">
            View and manage doctor accounts
          </p>
        </div>

        <Link
          to="/admin/doctors/add"
          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800"
        >
          <Plus size={18} />
          Add Doctor
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border bg-white px-4 py-3">
        <Search size={19} className="text-gray-400" />

        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search by name, email, specialization or license..."
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full outline-none"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border bg-white">
        {isLoading ? (
          <p className="p-8 text-center text-gray-500">
            Loading doctors...
          </p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Doctor Name</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Email</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Specialization</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">License Number</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody className="divide-y">
              {filteredDoctors.length > 0 ? (
                filteredDoctors.map((doctor) => (
                  <tr key={doctor.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                    <td className="px-5 py-4 font-medium text-gray-800">
                      {doctor.user?.firstName} {doctor.user?.lastName}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-5 py-4 text-gray-600">
                      {doctor.user?.email || "N/A"}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-5 py-4 text-gray-600">
                      {doctor.specialization || "N/A"}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-5 py-4 text-gray-600">
                      {doctor.licenseNumber || "N/A"}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-5 py-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <Link
                          to={`/admin/doctors/edit/${doctor.id}`}
                          title="Edit doctor"
                          className="text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
                        >
                          <Pencil size={18} />
                        </Link>

                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleDeactivate(doctor.id)}
                          title="Deactivate doctor"
                          className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800"
                        >
                          <UserX size={18} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))
              ) : (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan="5"
                    className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-gray-500"
                  >
                    No doctors found
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>

      <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
        Total doctors: {filteredDoctors.length}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminEditMedicalRecordPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function AdminEditMedicalRecordPage() {
  const { recordId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  const headers = { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };

  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    diagnosis: "",
    treatment: "",
    prescription: "",
    notes: "",
  });

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchRecord() {
      try {
        const response = await api.get(
          `/medical-records/${recordId}`,
          { headers }
        );

        const record = response.data.medicalRecord;

        setFormData({
          diagnosis: record.diagnosis || "",
          treatment: record.treatment || "",
          prescription: record.prescription || "",
          notes: record.notes || "",
        });
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load medical record"
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchRecord();
  }, [recordId]);

  function handleChange(e) {
    setFormData({
      ...formData,
      [e.target.name]: e.target.value,
    });
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    try {
      await api.put(
        `/medical-records/${recordId}`,
        formData,
        { headers }
      );

      toast.success("Medical record updated successfully");
      navigate(`/admin/medical-records/${recordId}`);
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to update medical record"
      );
    }
  }

  if (loading) {
    return <p className="text-gray-500">Loading medical record...</p>;
  }

  return (
    <div className="max-w-3xl">
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
          Edit Medical Record
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
          Update medical record information
        </p>
      </div>

      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        className="space-y-5 rounded-xl border bg-white p-6"
      >
        {[
          { name: "diagnosis", label: "Diagnosis", required: true },
          { name: "treatment", label: "Treatment" },
          { name: "prescription", label: "Prescription" },
          { name: "notes", label: "Notes" },
        ].map((field) => (
          <div key={field.name}>
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              {field.label}
            </label>

            <textarea
              name={field.name}
              value={formData[field.name]}
              onChange={handleChange}
              required={field.required}
              rows="3"
              className="w-full rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
              placeholder={`Enter ${field.label.toLowerCase()}`}
            />
          </div>
        ))}

        <div className="flex gap-3 pt-2">
          <button
            type="submit"
            className="rounded-lg bg-blue-700 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800"
          >
            Update Record
          </button>

          <Link
            to={`/admin/medical-records/${recordId}`}
            className="rounded-lg border px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-50"
          >
            Cancel
          </Link>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminInvoiceDetailsPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function AdminInvoiceDetailsPage() {
  const { invoiceId } = useParams();

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  const headers = { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };

  const [invoice, setInvoice] = useState(null);
  const [paymentInfo, setPaymentInfo] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);
  const [paymentAmount, setPaymentAmount] = useState("");
  const [submitting, setSubmitting] = useState(false);

  async function fetchInvoiceData() {
    try {
      const [invoiceResponse, paymentsResponse] = await Promise.all([
        api.get(`/invoices/${invoiceId}`, { headers }),
        api.get(`/invoices/${invoiceId}/payments`, { headers }),
      ]);

      setInvoice(invoiceResponse.data.invoice);
      setPaymentInfo(paymentsResponse.data);
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load invoice details"
      );
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchInvoiceData();
  }, [invoiceId]);

  async function handleAddPayment(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    const amount = Number(paymentAmount);

    if (!Number.isFinite(amount) || amount <= 0) {
      toast.error("Please enter a valid payment amount");
      return;
    }

    if (amount > Number(paymentInfo?.remainingAmount || 0)) {
      toast.error("Payment cannot exceed the remaining amount");
      return;
    }

    setSubmitting(true);

    try {
      await api.patch(
        `/invoices/${invoiceId}/payment`,
        { amount },
        { headers }
      );

      toast.success("Payment added successfully");
      setPaymentAmount("");
      await fetchInvoiceData();
    } catch (error) {
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to add payment"
      );
    } finally {
      setSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  function formatAmount(amount) {
    return Number(amount || 0).toFixed(2);
  }

  if (loading) {
    return <p className="text-gray-500">Loading invoice...</p>;
  }

  if (!invoice || !paymentInfo) {
    return (
      <div>
        <p className="text-gray-600">Invoice not found.</p>
        <Link
          to="/admin/billing"
          className="mt-4 inline-block text-blue-600"
        >
          Back to Billing
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const remainingAmount = Number(paymentInfo.remainingAmount || 0);

  return (
    <div className="max-w-5xl">
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
          Invoice Details
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
          View invoice information and payment history
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6 grid gap-4 sm:grid-cols-3">
        <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-5">
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Total Amount</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
            {formatAmount(paymentInfo.totalAmount)}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-5">
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Paid Amount</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-green-700">
            {formatAmount(paymentInfo.paidAmount)}
          </p>
        </div>

        <div className="rounded-xl border bg-white p-5">
          <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Remaining Amount</p>
          <p className="mt-2 text-2xl font-bold text-red-600">
            {formatAmount(remainingAmount)}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6 rounded-xl border bg-white p-6">
        <h2 className="mb-5 text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800">
          Invoice Information
        </h2>

        <div className="grid gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
          <div>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Patient</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-medium text-gray-800">
              {invoice.patient?.firstName} {invoice.patient?.lastName}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Appointment Date</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-medium text-gray-800">
              {invoice.appointment?.appointmentDate
                ? new Date(
                    invoice.appointment.appointmentDate
                  ).toLocaleString()
                : "-"}
            </p>
          </div>

          <div>
            <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">Invoice Status</p>
            <p className="mt-1 font-medium text-gray-800">
              {invoice.status?.replaceAll("_", " ")}
            </p>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      {remainingAmount > 0 && (
        <div className="mb-6 rounded-xl border bg-white p-6">
          <h2 className="mb-4 text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800">
            Add Payment
          </h2>

          <form
            onSubmit={handleAddPayment}
            className="flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row"
          >
            <input
              type="number"
              value={paymentAmount}
              onChange={(e) => setPaymentAmount(e.target.value)}
              min="0.01"
              max={remainingAmount}
              step="0.01"
              required
              placeholder="Enter payment amount"
              className="flex-1 rounded-lg border px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
            />

            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={submitting}
              className="rounded-lg bg-blue-700 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800 disabled:opacity-60"
            >
              {submitting ? "Adding..." : "Add Payment"}
            </button>
          </form>
        </div>
      )}

      <div className="mb-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border bg-white">
        <div className="border-b p-5">
          <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-800">
            Payment History
          </h2>
        </div>

        {!paymentInfo.payments || paymentInfo.payments.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="p-5 text-sm text-gray-500">
            No payments recorded.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600">
              <tr>
                <th className="p-4">Payment Date</th>
                <th className="p-4">Amount</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {paymentInfo.payments.map((payment) => (
                <tr key={payment.id} className="border-t">
                  <td className="p-4">
                    {payment.paymentDate
                      ? new Date(payment.paymentDate).toLocaleString()
                      : payment.createdAt
                        ? new Date(payment.createdAt).toLocaleString()
                        : "-"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4 font-medium">
                    {formatAmount(payment.amount)}
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>

      <Link
        to="/admin/billing"
        className="inline-block text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
      >
        Back to Billing
      </Link>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { Pencil } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function AdminMedicalRecordDetailsPage() {
  const { recordId } = useParams();

  const [record, setRecord] = useState(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  const headers = { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchRecord() {
      try {
        const response = await api.get(
          `/medical-records/${recordId}`,
          { headers }
        );

        setRecord(response.data.medicalRecord);
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load medical record"
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchRecord();
  }, [recordId]);

  if (loading) {
    return <p className="text-gray-500">Loading medical record...</p>;
  }

  if (!record) {
    return (
      <div>
        <p className="text-gray-600">Medical record not found.</p>
        <Link
          to="/admin/medical-records"
          className="mt-4 inline-block text-blue-600"
        >
          Back to Medical Records
        </Link>
      </div>
    );
  }

  const patientName = `${record.patient?.firstName || ""} ${
    record.patient?.lastName || ""
  }`;

  const doctorName = `${record.doctor?.user?.firstName || ""} ${
    record.doctor?.user?.lastName || ""
  }`;

  const details = [
    { label: "Patient", value: patientName },
    { label: "Doctor", value: `Dr. ${doctorName}` },
    {
      label: "Record Date",
      value: record.recordDate
        ? new Date(record.recordDate).toLocaleDateString()
        : "-",
    },
    { label: "Diagnosis", value: record.diagnosis },
    { label: "Treatment", value: record.treatment },
    { label: "Prescription", value: record.prescription },
    { label: "Notes", value: record.notes },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="max-w-4xl">
      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center justify-between">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
            Medical Record Details
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
            View patient medical information
          </p>
        </div>

        <Link
          to={`/admin/medical-records/edit/${record.id}`}
          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-700 px-4 py-2 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800"
        >
          <Pencil size={17} />
          Edit Record
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="space-y-5 rounded-xl border bg-white p-6">
        {details.map((item) => (
          <div key={item.label} className="border-b pb-4 last:border-0">
            <p className="mb-2 text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
              {item.label}
            </p>
            <p className="whitespace-pre-wrap text-gray-800">
              {item.value || "-"}
            </p>
          </div>
        ))}

        <Link
          to="/admin/medical-records"
          className="inline-block text-sm font-medium text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
        >
          Back to Medical Records
        </Link>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Eye, Search } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function AdminMedicalRecordsPage() {
  const [records, setRecords] = useState([]);
  const [patients, setPatients] = useState([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [patientFilter, setPatientFilter] = useState("");
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(true);

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  const headers = { Authorization: `Bearer ${token}` };

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchData() {
      try {
        const [recordsResponse, patientsResponse] = await Promise.all([
          api.get("/medical-records", { headers }),
          api.get("/patients", { headers }),
        ]);

        setRecords(recordsResponse.data.medicalRecords || []);
        setPatients(patientsResponse.data.patients || []);
      } catch (error) {
        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load medical records"
        );
      } finally {
        setLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchData();
  }, []);

  const filteredRecords = records.filter((record) => {
    const patientName =
      `${record.patient?.firstName || ""} ${record.patient?.lastName || ""}`.toLowerCase();

    const searchMatch =
      patientName.includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      (record.diagnosis || "").toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase());

    const patientMatch =
      !patientFilter || record.patientId === patientFilter;

    return searchMatch && patientMatch;
  });

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-gray-800">
          Medical Records
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
          View and manage patient medical records
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-5 flex flex-col gap-3 sm:flex-row">
        <div className="relative flex-1">
          <Search
            size={18}
            className="absolute left-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 text-gray-400"
          />
          <input
            type="text"
            value={search}
            onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
            placeholder="Search patient or diagnosis..."
            className="w-full rounded-lg border bg-white py-3 pl-10 pr-4 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
          />
        </div>

        <select
          value={patientFilter}
          onChange={(e) => setPatientFilter(e.target.value)}
          className="rounded-lg border bg-white px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-600"
        >
          <option value="">All Patients</option>
          {patients.map((patient) => (
            <option key={patient.id} value={patient.id}>
              {patient.firstName} {patient.lastName}
            </option>
          ))}
        </select>
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border bg-white">
        {loading ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">Loading medical records...</p>
        ) : filteredRecords.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">No medical records found.</p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600">
              <tr>
                <th className="p-4">Patient</th>
                <th className="p-4">Doctor</th>
                <th className="p-4">Diagnosis</th>
                <th className="p-4">Record Date</th>
                <th className="p-4">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {filteredRecords.map((record) => (
                <tr key={record.id} className="border-t">
                  <td className="p-4">
                    {record.patient?.firstName}{" "}
                    {record.patient?.lastName}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    Dr. {record.doctor?.user?.firstName}{" "}
                    {record.doctor?.user?.lastName}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    <span className="line-clamp-2">
                      {record.diagnosis}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {record.recordDate
                      ? new Date(record.recordDate).toLocaleDateString()
                      : "-"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    <Link
                      to={`/admin/medical-records/${record.id}`}
                      className="inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
                    >
                      <Eye size={18} />
                      View
                    </Link>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import {
  NavLink,
  Route,
  Routes,
  Navigate,
  useNavigate,
} from "react-router-dom";

import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Users,
  Stethoscope,
  CalendarDays,
  FileText,
  Receipt,
  LogOut,
} from "lucide-react";

import toast from "react-hot-toast";

import Dashboard from "./dashboardPage.jsx";
import AdminPatientsPage from "./adminPatientsPage.jsx";
import AdminPatientFormPage from "./adminPatientFormPage.jsx";
import AdminDoctorsPage from "./adminDoctorsPage.jsx";
import AdminDoctorFormPage from "./adminDoctorFormPage.jsx";
import AdminAppointmentsPage from "./adminAppointmentsPage.jsx";
import AdminAppointmentFormPage from "./adminAppointmentFormPage.jsx";
import AdminMedicalRecordsPage from "./adminMedicalRecordsPage.jsx";
import AdminMedicalRecordDetailsPage from "./adminMedicalRecordDetailsPage.jsx";
import AdminEditMedicalRecordPage from "./adminEditMedicalRecordPage.jsx";
import AdminBillingPage from "./adminBillingPage.jsx";
import AdminCreateInvoicePage from "./adminCreateInvoicePage.jsx";
import AdminInvoiceDetailsPage from "./adminInvoiceDetailsPage.jsx";

export default function AdminPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");
  let user = null;

  try {
    user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("user"));
  } catch {
    user = null;
  }

  if (!token || !user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  if (user.role !== "ADMIN") {
    if (user.role === "RECEPTIONIST") {
      return <Navigate to="/receptionist" replace />;
    }

    if (user.role === "DOCTOR") {
      return <Navigate to="/doctor" replace />;
    }

    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  function handleLogout() {
    localStorage.removeItem("token");
    localStorage.removeItem("user");

    toast.success("Logged out successfully");
    navigate("/login", { replace: true });
  }

  const menuItems = [
    { title: "Dashboard", path: "/admin", icon: LayoutDashboard, end: true },
    { title: "Patients", path: "/admin/patients", icon: Users },
    { title: "Doctors", path: "/admin/doctors", icon: Stethoscope },
    { title: "Appointments", path: "/admin/appointments", icon: CalendarDays },
    { title: "Medical Records", path: "/admin/medical-records", icon: FileText },
    { title: "Billing", path: "/admin/billing", icon: Receipt },
  ];

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full">
      <aside className="flex w-64 shrink-0 flex-col border-r bg-white p-5">
        <h1 className="mb-10 text-xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Hospital MS
        </h1>

        <nav className="flex-1 space-y-2">
          {menuItems.map((item) => {
            const Icon = item.icon;

            return (
              <NavLink
                key={item.path}
                to={item.path}
                end={item.end}
                className={({ isActive }) =>
                  `flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium ${
                    isActive
                      ? "bg-blue-100 text-blue-900"
                      : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
                  }`
                }
              >
                <Icon size={19} />
                {item.title}
              </NavLink>
            );
          })}
        </nav>

        <button
          onClick={handleLogout}
          className="mt-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50"
        >
          <LogOut size={19} />
          Logout
        </button>
      </aside>

      <main className="min-w-0 flex-1 bg-gray-50 p-8">
        <Routes>
          <Route path="/" element={<Dashboard />} />

          <Route path="patients" element={<AdminPatientsPage />} />
          <Route path="patients/add" element={<AdminPatientFormPage />} />
          <Route
            path="patients/edit/:patientId"
            element={<AdminPatientFormPage />}
          />

          <Route path="doctors" element={<AdminDoctorsPage />} />
          <Route path="doctors/add" element={<AdminDoctorFormPage />} />
          <Route
            path="doctors/edit/:doctorId"
            element={<AdminDoctorFormPage />}
          />

          <Route path="appointments" element={<AdminAppointmentsPage />} />
          <Route
            path="appointments/add"
            element={<AdminAppointmentFormPage />}
          />
          <Route
            path="appointments/edit/:appointmentId"
            element={<AdminAppointmentFormPage />}
          />

          <Route
            path="medical-records"
            element={<AdminMedicalRecordsPage />}
          />
          <Route
            path="medical-records/edit/:recordId"
            element={<AdminEditMedicalRecordPage />}
          />
          <Route
            path="medical-records/:recordId"
            element={<AdminMedicalRecordDetailsPage />}
          />

          <Route path="billing" element={<AdminBillingPage />} />
          <Route
            path="billing/add"
            element={<AdminCreateInvoicePage />}
          />
          <Route
            path="billing/:invoiceId"
            element={<AdminInvoiceDetailsPage />}
          />
        </Routes>
      </main>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminPatientFormPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link, useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import { ArrowLeft } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

import api from "../lib/api.js";

export default function AdminPatientFormPage() {
  const { patientId } = useParams();
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const isEdit = Boolean(patientId);

  const [formData, setFormData] = useState({
    firstName: "",
    lastName: "",
    dateOfBirth: "",
    phone: "",
    email: "",
    address: "",
  });

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(isEdit);
  const [isSubmitting, setIsSubmitting] = useState(false);

  useEffect(() => {
    if (isEdit) {
      fetchPatient();
    }
  }, [patientId]);

  async function fetchPatient() {
    try {
      const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

      const response = await api.get(`/patients/${patientId}`, {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      });

      const patient = response.data.patient;

      setFormData({
        firstName: patient.firstName || "",
        lastName: patient.lastName || "",
        dateOfBirth: patient.dateOfBirth
          ? new Date(patient.dateOfBirth).toISOString().split("T")[0]
          : "",
        phone: patient.phone || "",
        email: patient.email || "",
        address: patient.address || "",
      });
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Fetch patient error:", error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load patient"
      );
      navigate("/admin/patients");
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }

  function handleChange(e) {
    const { name, value } = e.target;

    setFormData((previous) => ({
      ...previous,
      [name]: value,
    }));
  }

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!formData.firstName.trim() || !formData.lastName.trim()) {
      toast.error("First name and last name are required");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setIsSubmitting(true);

      const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

      const config = {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      };

      const data = {
        ...formData,
        dateOfBirth: formData.dateOfBirth || null,
      };

      if (isEdit) {
        await api.put(`/patients/${patientId}`, data, config);
        toast.success("Patient updated successfully");
      } else {
        await api.post("/patients", data, config);
        toast.success("Patient added successfully");
      }

      navigate("/admin/patients");
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Save patient error:", error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to save patient"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSubmitting(false);
    }
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <p className="py-10 text-center text-gray-500">
        Loading patient details...
      </p>
    );
  }

  const inputClass =
    "mt-2 w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-700";

  return (
    <div>
      <Link
        to="/admin/patients"
        className="mb-6 inline-flex items-center gap-2 text-sm font-medium text-blue-900 hover:text-blue-700"
      >
        <ArrowLeft size={18} />
        Back to Patients
      </Link>

      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-800">
          {isEdit ? "Edit Patient" : "Add Patient"}
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-2 text-gray-500">
          {isEdit
            ? "Update patient information"
            : "Enter the new patient information"}
        </p>
      </div>

      <form
        onSubmit={handleSubmit}
        className="max-w-3xl space-y-6 rounded-xl border bg-white p-6"
      >
        <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 md:grid-cols-2">
          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              First Name *
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="firstName"
              value={formData.firstName}
              onChange={handleChange}
              required
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter first name"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Last Name *
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="lastName"
              value={formData.lastName}
              onChange={handleChange}
              required
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter last name"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Date of Birth
            </label>
            <input
              type="date"
              name="dateOfBirth"
              value={formData.dateOfBirth}
              onChange={handleChange}
              className={inputClass}
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Phone Number
            </label>
            <input
              type="tel"
              name="phone"
              value={formData.phone}
              onChange={handleChange}
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter phone number"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Email
            </label>
            <input
              type="email"
              name="email"
              value={formData.email}
              onChange={handleChange}
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter email address"
            />
          </div>

          <div>
            <label className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Address
            </label>
            <input
              type="text"
              name="address"
              value={formData.address}
              onChange={handleChange}
              className={inputClass}
              placeholder="Enter address"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3 border-t pt-5">
          <button
            type="submit"
            disabled={isSubmitting}
            className="rounded-lg bg-blue-900 px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800 disabled:opacity-50"
          >
            {isSubmitting
              ? "Saving..."
              : isEdit
                ? "Update Patient"
                : "Add Patient"}
          </button>

          <Link
            to="/admin/patients"
            className="rounded-lg border px-6 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100"
          >
            Cancel
          </Link>
        </div>
      </form>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\adminPatientsPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { Link } from "react-router-dom";
import { Plus, Search, Pencil, UserX } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";

import api from "../lib/api.js";

export default function AdminPatientsPage() {
  const [patients, setPatients] = useState([]);
  const [search, setSearch] = useState("");
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  async function fetchPatients() {
    try {
      const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

      const response = await api.get("/patients", {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      });

      setPatients(response.data.patients || []);
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Fetch patients error:", error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to load patients"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchPatients();
  }, []);

  async function handleDeactivate(patientId) {
    const confirmed = window.confirm(
      "Are you sure you want to deactivate this patient?"
    );

    if (!confirmed) return;

    try {
      const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

      await api.delete(`/patients/${patientId}`, {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      });

      toast.success("Patient deactivated successfully");

      setPatients((previous) =>
        previous.filter((patient) => patient.id !== patientId)
      );
    } catch (error) {
      console.error("Deactivate patient error:", error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message || "Failed to deactivate patient"
      );
    }
  }

  const filteredPatients = patients.filter((patient) => {
    const fullName =
      `${patient.firstName} ${patient.lastName}`.toLowerCase();

    return (
      fullName.includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      patient.email?.toLowerCase().includes(search.toLowerCase()) ||
      patient.phone?.includes(search)
    );
  });

  function formatDate(date) {
    if (!date) return "N/A";

    return new Date(date).toLocaleDateString();
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-8 flex flex-wrap items-center justify-between gap-4">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-800">
            Patient Management
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-gray-500">
            View and manage patient information
          </p>
        </div>

        <Link
          to="/admin/patients/add"
          className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-900 px-4 py-3 text-sm font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-800"
        >
          <Plus size={18} />
          Add Patient
        </Link>
      </div>

      <div className="mb-6 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg border bg-white px-4 py-3">
        <Search size={19} className="text-gray-400" />

        <input
          type="text"
          placeholder="Search by name, email or phone..."
          value={search}
          onChange={(e) => setSearch(e.target.value)}
          className="w-full outline-none"
        />
      </div>

      <div className="overflow-x-auto rounded-xl border bg-white">
        {isLoading ? (
          <p className="p-8 text-center text-gray-500">
            Loading patients...
          </p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-600">
              <tr>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Patient Name</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Date of Birth</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Phone</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Email</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Address</th>
                <th className="px-5 py-4">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody className="divide-y">
              {filteredPatients.length > 0 ? (
                filteredPatients.map((patient) => (
                  <tr key={patient.id} className="hover:bg-gray-50">
                    <td className="px-5 py-4 font-medium text-gray-800">
                      {patient.firstName} {patient.lastName}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-5 py-4 text-gray-600">
                      {formatDate(patient.dateOfBirth)}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-5 py-4 text-gray-600">
                      {patient.phone || "N/A"}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-5 py-4 text-gray-600">
                      {patient.email || "N/A"}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-5 py-4 text-gray-600">
                      {patient.address || "N/A"}
                    </td>

                    <td className="px-5 py-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-3">
                        <Link
                          to={`/admin/patients/edit/${patient.id}`}
                          title="Edit patient"
                          className="text-blue-600 hover:text-blue-800"
                        >
                          <Pencil size={18} />
                        </Link>

                        <button
                          onClick={() => handleDeactivate(patient.id)}
                          title="Deactivate patient"
                          className="text-red-600 hover:text-red-800"
                        >
                          <UserX size={18} />
                        </button>
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                ))
              ) : (
                <tr>
                  <td
                    colSpan="6"
                    className="px-5 py-10 text-center text-gray-500"
                  >
                    No patients found
                  </td>
                </tr>
              )}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>

      <p className="mt-4 text-sm text-gray-500">
        Total patients: {filteredPatients.length}
      </p>
    </div>
  );
}
