========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\dashboardPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import {
  Users,
  Stethoscope,
  CalendarDays,
  FileText,
} from "lucide-react";

import toast from "react-hot-toast";

import StatCard from "../components/statCard.jsx";
import api from "../lib/api.js";

export default function Dashboard() {
  const [stats, setStats] = useState({
    patients: 0,
    doctors: 0,
    appointments: 0,
    medicalRecords: 0,
  });

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchDashboardData() {
      try {
        const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

        const config = {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        };

        const [
          patientsResponse,
          doctorsResponse,
          appointmentsResponse,
          recordsResponse,
        ] = await Promise.all([
          api.get("/patients", config),
          api.get("/doctors", config),
          api.get("/appointments", config),
          api.get("/medical-records", config),
        ]);

        setStats({
          patients:
            patientsResponse.data.patients?.length ?? 0,

          doctors:
            doctorsResponse.data.doctors?.length ?? 0,

          appointments:
            appointmentsResponse.data.appointments?.length ?? 0,

          medicalRecords:
            recordsResponse.data.medicalRecords?.length ?? 0,
        });
      } catch (error) {
        console.error("Dashboard data error:", error);

        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message ||
            "Failed to load dashboard data"
        );
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchDashboardData();
  }, []);

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Page Header */}
      <div className="mb-8">
        <h1 className="text-3xl font-bold text-gray-800">
          Dashboard
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-2 text-gray-500">
          Welcome to Hospital Management System
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Statistics */}
      <div className="grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-2 xl:grid-cols-4">
        <StatCard
          title="Total Patients"
          value={isLoading ? "..." : stats.patients}
          icon={Users}
          color="bg-blue-100 text-blue-600"
        />

        <StatCard
          title="Total Doctors"
          value={isLoading ? "..." : stats.doctors}
          icon={Stethoscope}
          color="bg-green-100 text-green-600"
        />

        <StatCard
          title="Appointments"
          value={isLoading ? "..." : stats.appointments}
          icon={CalendarDays}
          color="bg-purple-100 text-purple-600"
        />

        <StatCard
          title="Medical Records"
          value={isLoading ? "..." : stats.medicalRecords}
          icon={FileText}
          color="bg-orange-100 text-orange-600"
        />
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\doctorAddMedicalRecordPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function DoctorAddMedicalRecordPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);
  const [selectedAppointment, setSelectedAppointment] =
    useState("");

  const [diagnosis, setDiagnosis] = useState("");
  const [treatment, setTreatment] = useState("");
  const [prescription, setPrescription] = useState("");
  const [notes, setNotes] = useState("");

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

  // Fetch Appointments and Medical Records
  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchData() {
      try {
        const [appointmentRes, recordRes] = await Promise.all([
          api.get("/appointments", {
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
          }),

          api.get("/medical-records", {
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
          }),
        ]);

        const allAppointments =
          appointmentRes.data.appointments || [];

        const medicalRecords =
          recordRes.data.medicalRecords || [];

        const availableAppointments = allAppointments.filter(
          (appointment) =>
            appointment.status !== "CANCELLED" &&
            !medicalRecords.some(
              (record) =>
                record.appointmentId === appointment.id
            )
        );

        setAppointments(availableAppointments);
      } catch (error) {
        console.log(error);

        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message ||
          "Failed to load appointments"
        );
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchData();
  }, [token]);

  // Create Medical Record
  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!selectedAppointment || !diagnosis.trim()) {
      toast.error(
        "Please select an appointment and enter diagnosis"
      );
      return;
    }

    const appointment = appointments.find(
      (item) => item.id === selectedAppointment
    );

    if (!appointment) {
      toast.error("Invalid appointment selected");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setIsSaving(true);

      await api.post(
        "/medical-records",
        {
          patientId: appointment.patientId,
          appointmentId: appointment.id,
          diagnosis: diagnosis.trim(),
          treatment: treatment.trim(),
          prescription: prescription.trim(),
          notes: notes.trim(),
        },
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        }
      );

      toast.success("Medical record created successfully");

      navigate("/doctor/medical-records");
    } catch (error) {
      console.log(error);

      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message ||
        "Failed to create medical record"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Page Header */}
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Add Medical Record
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
          Create a new medical record for your patient.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Form */}
      <div className="max-w-3xl rounded-lg bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>

          {/* Appointment */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Select Appointment *
            </label>

            <select
              value={selectedAppointment}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setSelectedAppointment(e.target.value)
              }
              required
              disabled={isLoading}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            >
              <option value="">
                {isLoading
                  ? "Loading appointments..."
                  : "Select an appointment"}
              </option>

              {appointments.map((appointment) => (
                <option
                  key={appointment.id}
                  value={appointment.id}
                >
                  {appointment.patient?.firstName}{" "}
                  {appointment.patient?.lastName}
                  {" - "}
                  {new Date(
                    appointment.appointmentDate
                  ).toLocaleString()}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>

            {!isLoading && appointments.length === 0 && (
              <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-500">
                No available appointments.
              </p>
            )}
          </div>

          {/* Diagnosis */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Diagnosis *
            </label>

            <textarea
              value={diagnosis}
              onChange={(e) => setDiagnosis(e.target.value)}
              required
              rows={3}
              placeholder="Enter diagnosis"
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Treatment */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Treatment
            </label>

            <textarea
              value={treatment}
              onChange={(e) => setTreatment(e.target.value)}
              rows={3}
              placeholder="Enter treatment details"
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Prescription */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Prescription
            </label>

            <textarea
              value={prescription}
              onChange={(e) =>
                setPrescription(e.target.value)
              }
              rows={3}
              placeholder="Enter prescription details"
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Notes */}
          <div className="mb-6">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Additional Notes
            </label>

            <textarea
              value={notes}
              onChange={(e) => setNotes(e.target.value)}
              rows={3}
              placeholder="Enter additional notes"
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Buttons */}
          <div className="flex gap-3">
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={
                isSaving ||
                isLoading ||
                appointments.length === 0
              }
              className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {isSaving ? "Saving..." : "Save Medical Record"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() =>
                navigate("/doctor/medical-records")
              }
              disabled={isSaving}
              className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-6 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
          </div>

        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function DoctorAppointmentsPage() {
  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

    api.get("/appointments", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
    })
      .then((res) => {
        setAppointments(res.data.appointments);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.log(err);
        setError(
          err.response?.data?.message ||
          "Failed to load appointments"
        );
      })
      .finally(() => {
        setIsLoading(false);
      });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div>
      <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
        My Appointments
      </h1>

      <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
        View your assigned patient appointments.
      </p>

      <div className="mt-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-lg bg-white shadow">
        {isLoading ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">
            Loading appointments...
          </p>
        ) : error ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-red-600">{error}</p>
        ) : appointments.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">
            No appointments found.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-700">
              <tr>
                <th className="p-4">Patient</th>
                <th className="p-4">Date & Time</th>
                <th className="p-4">Reason</th>
                <th className="p-4">Status</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {appointments.map((appointment) => (
                <tr
                  key={appointment.id}
                  className="border-t hover:bg-gray-50"
                >
                  <td className="p-4 font-medium">
                    {appointment.patient.firstName}{" "}
                    {appointment.patient.lastName}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {new Date(
                      appointment.appointmentDate
                    ).toLocaleString()}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {appointment.reason || "-"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    <span className="rounded-full bg-blue-100 px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium text-blue-700">
                      {appointment.status}
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\doctorPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";

import {
  Link,
  Navigate,
  useNavigate,
  Routes,
  Route,
  useLocation,
} from "react-router-dom";

import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  CalendarDays,
  FileText,
  LogOut,
} from "lucide-react";

import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

import DoctorAppointmentsPage from "./doctorAppointmentsPage";
import DoctorMedicalRecordsPage from "./doctorMedicalRecordsPage";
import DoctorAddMedicalRecordPage from "./doctorAddMedicalRecordPage";

export default function DoctorPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);
  const [medicalRecords, setMedicalRecords] = useState([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

  let user = null;

  try {
    user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("user"));
  } catch {
    user = null;
  }

  // Check authentication
  if (!token || !user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  // Check doctor role
  if (user.role !== "DOCTOR") {
    if (user.role === "ADMIN") {
      return <Navigate to="/admin" replace />;
    }

    if (user.role === "RECEPTIONIST") {
      return <Navigate to="/receptionist" replace />;
    }

    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  // Fetch dashboard data
  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchDashboardData() {
      try {
        const [appointmentRes, medicalRecordRes] =
          await Promise.all([
            api.get("/appointments", {
              headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
              },
            }),

            api.get("/medical-records", {
              headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
              },
            }),
          ]);

        setAppointments(
          appointmentRes.data.appointments || []
        );

        setMedicalRecords(
          medicalRecordRes.data.medicalRecords || []
        );
      } catch (error) {
        console.log("Dashboard data error:", error);

        toast.error(
          "Failed to load dashboard statistics"
        );
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchDashboardData();
  }, [token]);

  // Calculate statistics
  const completedAppointments = appointments.filter(
    (appointment) => appointment.status === "COMPLETED"
  ).length;

  // Logout
  function handleLogout() {
    localStorage.removeItem("token");
    localStorage.removeItem("user");

    toast.success("Logged out successfully");
    navigate("/login", { replace: true });
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full">

      {/* Sidebar */}
      <div className="flex w-64 flex-col border-r bg-white p-5">

        <h1 className="mb-10 text-xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Hospital MS
        </h1>

        {/* Dashboard */}
        <Link
          to="/doctor"
          className={`mb-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg p-3 ${
            location.pathname === "/doctor"
              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
              : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
          }`}
        >
          <LayoutDashboard size={20} />
          Dashboard
        </Link>

        {/* Appointments */}
        <Link
          to="/doctor/appointments"
          className={`mb-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg p-3 ${
            location.pathname === "/doctor/appointments"
              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
              : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
          }`}
        >
          <CalendarDays size={20} />
          Appointments
        </Link>

        {/* Medical Records */}
        <Link
          to="/doctor/medical-records"
          className={`mb-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg p-3 ${
            location.pathname.startsWith("/doctor/medical-records") ||
            location.pathname === "/doctor/add-medical-record"
              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
              : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
          }`}
        >
          <FileText size={20} />
          Medical Records
        </Link>

        {/* Logout */}
        <div className="mt-auto border-t pt-5">
          <button
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg p-3 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50"
          >
            <LogOut size={20} />
            Logout
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      {/* Main Content */}
      <div className="flex-1 bg-gray-50 p-8">

        <Routes>
          {/* Dashboard */}
          <Route
            path="/"
            element={
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
                  Doctor Dashboard
                </h1>

                <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
                  Welcome, {user.firstName} {user.lastName}.
                </p>

                {/* Statistics */}
                <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">

                  <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                          Total Appointments
                        </p>

                        <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-blue-900">
                          {isLoading ? "..." : appointments.length}
                        </h2>
                      </div>

                      <div className="rounded-lg bg-blue-100 p-3 text-blue-600">
                        <CalendarDays size={26} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                          Completed Appointments
                        </p>

                        <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-green-700">
                          {isLoading ? "..." : completedAppointments}
                        </h2>
                      </div>

                      <div className="rounded-lg bg-green-100 p-3 text-green-600">
                        <LayoutDashboard size={26} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm text-gray-500">
                          Total Medical Records
                        </p>

                          {isLoading ? "..." : medicalRecords.length}

========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\loginPage.jsx ==========


import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useState } from "react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function LoginPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  function handleLogin() {
    if (!email || !password) {
      toast.error("Please enter email and password");
      return;
    }

    api.post("/auth/login", {
      email: email,
      password: password,
    })
      .then((res) => {
        toast.success("Login successful");

        // Store JWT token
        localStorage.setItem("token", res.data.token);

        // Store logged-in user
        localStorage.setItem("user", JSON.stringify(res.data.user));

        console.log("Logged User:", res.data.user);

        // Navigate according to user role
        const role = res.data.user.role;

        if (role === "ADMIN") {
          navigate("/admin", { replace: true });
        } else if (role === "RECEPTIONIST") {
          navigate("/receptionist", { replace: true });
        } else if (role === "DOCTOR") {
          navigate("/doctor", { replace: true });
        } else {
          toast.error("Invalid user role");
        }
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.log(err);

        toast.error(
          err.response?.data?.message || "Login failed"
        );
      });
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full items-center justify-center bg-gray-50 px-4">
      <div className="flex w-full max-w-md flex-col items-center rounded-xl bg-white p-8 shadow-lg">

        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Hospital Management System
        </h1>

        <h2 className="mt-5 text-xl font-semibold text-gray-700">
          Login
        </h2>

        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
          Sign in to your account
        </p>

        <label className="mt-6 w-full font-medium text-gray-700">
          Email
        </label>

        <input
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          type="email"
          className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          placeholder="user@gmail.com"
        />

        <label className="mt-5 w-full font-medium text-gray-700">
          Password
        </label>

        <input
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          type="password"
          className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          placeholder="Enter your password"
        />

        <button
          onClick={handleLogin}
          className="mt-6 h-11 w-full rounded-lg bg-blue-600 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700"
        >
          Login
        </button>

        <p className="mt-5 w-full text-right text-sm text-gray-600">
          Don't have an account?{" "}
          <Link
            to="/register"
            className="font-semibold text-blue-600"
          >
            Register here
          </Link>
        </p>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function ReceptionistAddAppointmentPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [patients, setPatients] = useState([]);
  const [doctors, setDoctors] = useState([]);

  const [patientId, setPatientId] = useState("");
  const [doctorId, setDoctorId] = useState("");
  const [appointmentDate, setAppointmentDate] = useState("");
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchData() {
      try {
        const [patientRes, doctorRes] = await Promise.all([
          api.get("/patients", {
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
          }),
          api.get("/doctors", {
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
          }),
        ]);

        setPatients(patientRes.data.patients || []);
        setDoctors(doctorRes.data.doctors || []);
      } catch (error) {
        console.log(error);
        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message ||
          "Failed to load patients and doctors"
        );
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchData();
  }, [token]);

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!patientId || !doctorId || !appointmentDate) {
      toast.error("Please complete all required fields");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setIsSaving(true);

      await api.post(
        "/appointments",
        {
          patientId,
          doctorId,
          appointmentDate: new Date(appointmentDate).toISOString(),
          reason: reason.trim(),
        },
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        }
      );

      toast.success("Appointment created successfully");
      navigate("/receptionist/appointments");
    } catch (error) {
      console.log(error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message ||
        "Failed to create appointment"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Add Appointment
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
          Schedule an appointment for a patient.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-3xl rounded-lg bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        {isLoading ? (
          <p className="text-gray-500">
            Loading patients and doctors...
          </p>
        ) : (
          <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
            {/* Patient */}
            <div className="mb-5">
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                Select Patient *
              </label>

              <select
                value={patientId}
                onChange={(e) => setPatientId(e.target.value)}
                required
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              >
                <option value="">Select a patient</option>

                {patients.map((patient) => (
                  <option key={patient.id} value={patient.id}>
                    {patient.firstName} {patient.lastName}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>

              {patients.length === 0 && (
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-500">
                  No active patients available.
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Doctor */}
            <div className="mb-5">
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                Select Doctor *
              </label>

              <select
                value={doctorId}
                onChange={(e) => setDoctorId(e.target.value)}
                required
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              >
                <option value="">Select a doctor</option>

                {doctors.map((doctor) => (
                  <option key={doctor.id} value={doctor.id}>
                    Dr. {doctor.user?.firstName || doctor.firstName}{" "}
                    {doctor.user?.lastName || doctor.lastName}
                    {doctor.specialization
                      ? ` - ${doctor.specialization}`
                      : ""}
                  </option>
                ))}
              </select>

              {doctors.length === 0 && (
                <p className="mt-2 text-sm text-gray-500">
                  No active doctors available.
                </p>
              )}
            </div>

            {/* Appointment Date */}
            <div className="mb-5">
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                Appointment Date & Time *
              </label>

              <input
                type="datetime-local"
                value={appointmentDate}
                onChange={(e) => setAppointmentDate(e.target.value)}
                required
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Reason */}
            <div className="mb-6">
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                Reason
              </label>

              <textarea
                value={reason}
                onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
                rows={3}
                placeholder="Enter appointment reason"
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            {/* Buttons */}
            <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
              <button
                type="submit"
                disabled={
                  isSaving ||
                  patients.length === 0 ||
                  doctors.length === 0
                }
                className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:cursor-not-allowed disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {isSaving ? "Saving..." : "Save Appointment"}
              </button>

              <button
                type="button"
                onClick={() => navigate("/receptionist/appointments")}
                disabled={isSaving}
                className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-6 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100"
              >
                Cancel
              </button>
            </div>
          </form>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function ReceptionistAddPatientPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState("");
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState("");
  const [dateOfBirth, setDateOfBirth] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [address, setAddress] = useState("");

  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!firstName.trim() || !lastName.trim()) {
      toast.error("First name and last name are required");
      return;
    }

    const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

    try {
      setIsSaving(true);

      await api.post(
        "/patients",
        {
          firstName: firstName.trim(),
          lastName: lastName.trim(),
          dateOfBirth: dateOfBirth || null,
          phone: phone.trim(),
          email: email.trim(),
          address: address.trim(),
        },
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        }
      );

      toast.success("Patient created successfully");
      navigate("/receptionist/patients");
    } catch (error) {
      console.log(error);

      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message ||
        "Failed to create patient"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Page Header */}
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Add Patient
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
          Enter the patient's information below.
        </p>
      </div>

      {/* Form */}

                type="text"
                value={firstName}

                placeholder="Enter first name"
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                Last Name *
              </label>

              <input
                type="text"
                value={lastName}
                onChange={(e) => setLastName(e.target.value)}
                required
                placeholder="Enter last name"
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          {/* Date of Birth */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">

              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">

                onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}

========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\receptionistAppointmentsPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Plus, Pencil, X } from "lucide-react";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function ReceptionistAppointmentsPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");
  const [selectedAppointment, setSelectedAppointment] = useState(null);
  const [isCancelling, setIsCancelling] = useState(false);

  async function fetchAppointments() {
    const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

    try {
      setIsLoading(true);
      setError("");

      const res = await api.get("/appointments", {
        headers: {
          Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
        },
      });

      setAppointments(res.data.appointments || []);
    } catch (err) {
      console.log(err);
      setError(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        "Failed to load appointments"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsLoading(false);
    }
  }

  useEffect(() => {
    fetchAppointments();
  }, []);

  async function handleCancel() {
    if (!selectedAppointment) return;

    const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

    try {
      setIsCancelling(true);

      await api.patch(
        `/appointments/${selectedAppointment.id}/cancel`,
        {},
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        }
      );

      toast.success("Appointment cancelled successfully");
      setSelectedAppointment(null);
      fetchAppointments();
    } catch (err) {
      console.log(err);
      toast.error(
        err.response?.data?.message ||
        "Failed to cancel appointment"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsCancelling(false);
    }
  }

  function getStatusStyle(status) {
    const styles = {
      SCHEDULED: "bg-blue-100 text-blue-700",
      CONFIRMED: "bg-green-100 text-green-700",
      COMPLETED: "bg-purple-100 text-purple-700",
      CANCELLED: "bg-red-100 text-red-700",
    };

    return styles[status] || "bg-gray-100 text-gray-700";
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Page Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
            Appointments
          </h1>
          <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
            Manage patient appointments.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/receptionist/add-appointment")}
          className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-700"
        >
          <Plus size={20} />
          Add Appointment
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Appointments Table */}
      <div className="mt-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-lg bg-white shadow-sm">
        {isLoading ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">
            Loading appointments...
          </p>
        ) : error ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-red-600">{error}</p>
        ) : appointments.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">
            No appointments found.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-700">
              <tr>
                <th className="p-4">Patient</th>
                <th className="p-4">Doctor</th>
                <th className="p-4">Date & Time</th>
                <th className="p-4">Reason</th>
                <th className="p-4">Status</th>
                <th className="p-4">Actions</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {appointments.map((appointment) => {
                const doctor = appointment.doctor?.user;

                return (
                  <tr
                    key={appointment.id}
                    className="border-t hover:bg-gray-50"
                  >
                    <td className="p-4 font-medium">
                      {appointment.patient?.firstName}{" "}
                      {appointment.patient?.lastName}
                    </td>

                    <td className="p-4">
                      {doctor
                        ? `Dr. ${doctor.firstName} ${doctor.lastName}`
                        : "-"}
                    </td>

                    <td className="p-4">
                      {new Date(
                        appointment.appointmentDate
                      ).toLocaleString()}
                    </td>

                    <td className="p-4">
                      {appointment.reason || "-"}
                    </td>

                    <td className="p-4">
                      <span
                        className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${getStatusStyle(
                          appointment.status
                        )}`}
                      >
                        {appointment.status}
                      </span>
                    </td>

                    <td className="p-4">
                      <div className="flex items-center gap-2">
                        {appointment.status !== "CANCELLED" &&
                          appointment.status !== "COMPLETED" && (
                            <>
                              <button
                                onClick={() =>
                                  navigate(
                                    `/receptionist/edit-appointment/${appointment.id}`
                                  )
                                }
                                title="Edit appointment"
                                className="rounded-lg border border-blue-200 p-2 text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50"
                              >
                                <Pencil size={16} />
                              </button>

                              <button
                                onClick={() =>
                                  setSelectedAppointment(appointment)
                                }
                                title="Cancel appointment"
                                className="rounded-lg border border-red-200 p-2 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50"
                              >
                                <X size={16} />
                              </button>
                            </>
                          )}

                        {(appointment.status === "CANCELLED" ||
                          appointment.status === "COMPLETED") && (
                          <span className="text-xs text-gray-400">
                            No actions
                          </span>
                        )}
                      </div>
                    </td>
                  </tr>
                );
              })}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>

      {/* Cancel Confirmation Modal */}
      {selectedAppointment && (
        <div className="fixed inset-0 z-50 flex items-center justify-center bg-black/40 p-4">
          <div className="w-full max-w-md rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-lg">
            <h2 className="text-lg font-semibold text-gray-900">
              Cancel Appointment
            </h2>

            <p className="mt-3 text-sm text-gray-600">
              Are you sure you want to cancel this appointment?
              This will change its status to CANCELLED.
            </p>

            <div className="mt-6 flex justify-end gap-3">
              <button
                onClick={() => setSelectedAppointment(null)}
                disabled={isCancelling}
                className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-4 py-2 text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100"
              >
                No, Keep It
              </button>

              <button
                onClick={handleCancel}
                disabled={isCancelling}
                className="rounded-lg bg-red-600 px-4 py-2 font-medium text-white hover:bg-red-700 disabled:opacity-50"
              >
                {isCancelling ? "Cancelling..." : "Yes, Cancel"}
              </button>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\receptionistEditAppointmentPage.jsx ==========


import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function ReceptionistEditAppointmentPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { appointmentId } = useParams();

  const [patients, setPatients] = useState([]);
  const [doctors, setDoctors] = useState([]);

  const [patientId, setPatientId] = useState("");
  const [doctorId, setDoctorId] = useState("");
  const [appointmentDate, setAppointmentDate] = useState("");
  const [reason, setReason] = useState("");
  const [status, setStatus] = useState("SCHEDULED");

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchData() {
      try {
        const [appointmentRes, patientRes, doctorRes] =
          await Promise.all([
            api.get(`/appointments/${appointmentId}`, {
              headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
              },
            }),
            api.get("/patients", {
              headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
              },
            }),
            api.get("/doctors", {
              headers: {
                Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
              },
            }),
          ]);

        const appointment = appointmentRes.data.appointment;

        if (
          appointment.status === "CANCELLED" ||
          appointment.status === "COMPLETED"
        ) {
          toast.error("This appointment cannot be edited");
          navigate("/receptionist/appointments");
          return;
        }

        setPatients(patientRes.data.patients || []);
        setDoctors(doctorRes.data.doctors || []);

        setPatientId(appointment.patientId);
        setDoctorId(appointment.doctorId);

        setAppointmentDate(
          appointment.appointmentDate
            ? new Date(appointment.appointmentDate)
                .toISOString()
                .slice(0, 16)
            : ""
        );

        setReason(appointment.reason || "");
        setStatus(appointment.status);
      } catch (error) {
        console.log(error);
        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message ||
          "Failed to load appointment details"
        );
        navigate("/receptionist/appointments");
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchData();
  }, [appointmentId, token, navigate]);

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!patientId || !doctorId || !appointmentDate) {
      toast.error("Please complete all required fields");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setIsSaving(true);

      await api.put(
        `/appointments/${appointmentId}`,
        {
          patientId,
          doctorId,
          appointmentDate: new Date(appointmentDate).toISOString(),
          reason: reason.trim(),
          status,
        },
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        }
      );

      toast.success("Appointment updated successfully");
      navigate("/receptionist/appointments");
    } catch (error) {
      console.log(error);
      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message ||
        "Failed to update appointment"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return (
      <p className="text-gray-500">
        Loading appointment...
      </p>
    );
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Edit Appointment
        </h1>
        <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
          Update appointment information.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-3xl rounded-lg bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          {/* Patient */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Select Patient *
            </label>

            <select
              value={patientId}
              onChange={(e) => setPatientId(e.target.value)}
              required
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            >
              <option value="">Select a patient</option>

              {patients.map((patient) => (
                <option key={patient.id} value={patient.id}>
                  {patient.firstName} {patient.lastName}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Doctor */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Select Doctor *
            </label>

            <select
              value={doctorId}
              onChange={(e) => setDoctorId(e.target.value)}
              required
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            >
              <option value="">Select a doctor</option>

              {doctors.map((doctor) => (
                <option key={doctor.id} value={doctor.id}>
                  Dr. {doctor.user?.firstName || doctor.firstName}{" "}
                  {doctor.user?.lastName || doctor.lastName}
                  {doctor.specialization
                    ? ` - ${doctor.specialization}`
                    : ""}
                </option>
              ))}
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Appointment Date */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Appointment Date & Time *
            </label>

            <input
              type="datetime-local"
              value={appointmentDate}
              onChange={(e) => setAppointmentDate(e.target.value)}
              required
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Reason */}
          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Reason
            </label>

            <textarea
              value={reason}
              onChange={(e) => setReason(e.target.value)}
              rows={3}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          {/* Status */}
          <div className="mb-6">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Status
            </label>

            <select
              value={status}
              onChange={(e) => setStatus(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            >
              <option value="SCHEDULED">Scheduled</option>
              <option value="CONFIRMED">Confirmed</option>
              <option value="COMPLETED">Completed</option>
            </select>
          </div>

          {/* Buttons */}
          <div className="flex flex-wrap gap-3">
            <button
              type="submit"
              disabled={isSaving}
              className="rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-6 py-3 font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-700 disabled:opacity-50"
            >
              {isSaving ? "Updating..." : "Update Appointment"}
            </button>

            <button
              type="button"
              onClick={() => navigate("/receptionist/appointments")}
              disabled={isSaving}
              className="rounded-lg border border-gray-300 px-6 py-3 font-medium text-gray-700 hover:bg-gray-100"
            >
              Cancel
            </button>
          </div>
        </form>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate, useParams } from "react-router-dom";
import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function ReceptionistEditPatientPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const { patientId } = useParams();

  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState("");
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState("");
  const [dateOfBirth, setDateOfBirth] = useState("");
  const [phone, setPhone] = useState("");
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [address, setAddress] = useState("");

  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [isSaving, setIsSaving] = useState(false);

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchPatient() {
      try {
        const res = await api.get(`/patients/${patientId}`, {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        });

        const patient = res.data.patient;

        setFirstName(patient.firstName || "");
        setLastName(patient.lastName || "");
        setDateOfBirth(
          patient.dateOfBirth
            ? new Date(patient.dateOfBirth)
                .toISOString()
                .split("T")[0]
            : ""
        );
        setPhone(patient.phone || "");
        setEmail(patient.email || "");
        setAddress(patient.address || "");
      } catch (error) {
        console.log(error);

        toast.error(
          error.response?.data?.message ||
          "Failed to load patient"
        );

        navigate("/receptionist/patients");
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchPatient();
  }, [patientId, token, navigate]);

  async function handleSubmit(e) {
    e.preventDefault();

    if (!firstName.trim() || !lastName.trim()) {
      toast.error("First name and last name are required");
      return;
    }

    try {
      setIsSaving(true);

      await api.put(
        `/patients/${patientId}`,
        {
          firstName: firstName.trim(),
          lastName: lastName.trim(),
          dateOfBirth: dateOfBirth || null,
          phone: phone.trim(),
          email: email.trim(),
          address: address.trim(),
        },
        {
          headers: {
            Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
          },
        }
      );

      toast.success("Patient updated successfully");
      navigate("/receptionist/patients");
    } catch (error) {
      console.log(error);

      toast.error(
        error.response?.data?.message ||
        "Failed to update patient"
      );
    } finally {
      setIsSaving(false);
    }
  }

  if (isLoading) {
    return <p className="text-gray-500">Loading patient...</p>;
  }

  return (
    <div>
      <div className="mb-6">
        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Edit Patient
        </h1>

        <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
          Update the patient's information.
        </p>
      </div>

      <div className="max-w-3xl rounded-lg bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
        <form onSubmit={handleSubmit}>
          <div className="mb-5 grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div>
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                First Name *
              </label>

              <input
                type="text"
                value={firstName}
                onChange={(e) => setFirstName(e.target.value)}
                required
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                Last Name *
              </label>

              <input
                type="text"
                value={lastName}
                onChange={(e) => setLastName(e.target.value)}
                required
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>
          </div>

          <div className="mb-5">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
              Date of Birth
            </label>

            <input
              type="date"
              value={dateOfBirth}
              onChange={(e) => setDateOfBirth(e.target.value)}
              className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="mb-5 grid grid-cols-1 gap-5 sm:grid-cols-2">
            <div>
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                Phone
              </label>

              <input
                type="tel"
                value={phone}
                onChange={(e) => setPhone(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

            <div>
              <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">
                Email
              </label>

              <input
                type="email"
                value={email}
                onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
                className="w-full rounded-lg border border-gray-300 p-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              />
            </div>

          <div className="mb-6">
            <label className="mb-2 block text-sm font-medium text-gray-700">

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import {
  Link,
  Navigate,
  Route,
  Routes,
  useLocation,
  useNavigate,
} from "react-router-dom";

import {
  LayoutDashboard,
  Users,
  CalendarDays,
  Clock,
  LogOut,
} from "lucide-react";

import toast from "react-hot-toast";
import api from "../lib/api";

import ReceptionistPatientsPage from "./receptionistPatientsPage";
import ReceptionistAddPatientPage from "./receptionistAddPatientPage";
import ReceptionistEditPatientPage from "./receptionistEditPatientPage";

import ReceptionistAppointmentsPage from "./receptionistAppointmentsPage";
import ReceptionistAddAppointmentPage from "./receptionistAddAppointmentPage";
import ReceptionistEditAppointmentPage from "./receptionistEditAppointmentPage";

export default function ReceptionistPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  const location = useLocation();

  const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

  const [patients, setPatients] = useState([]);
  const [appointments, setAppointments] = useState([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);

  let user = null;

  try {
    user = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem("user"));
  } catch {
    user = null;
  }

  // Fetch Dashboard Data
  useEffect(() => {
    async function fetchDashboardData() {
      if (!token || !user || user.role !== "RECEPTIONIST") {
        setIsLoading(false);
        return;
      }

      try {
        const [patientRes, appointmentRes] = await Promise.all([
          api.get("/patients", {
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
          }),
          api.get("/appointments", {
            headers: {
              Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
            },
          }),
        ]);

        setPatients(patientRes.data.patients || []);
        setAppointments(appointmentRes.data.appointments || []);
      } catch (error) {
        console.log(error);
        toast.error("Failed to load dashboard data");
      } finally {
        setIsLoading(false);
      }
    }

    fetchDashboardData();
  }, [token]);

  // Authentication
  if (!token || !user) {
    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  // Role Protection
  if (user.role !== "RECEPTIONIST") {
    if (user.role === "ADMIN") {
      return <Navigate to="/admin" replace />;
    }

    if (user.role === "DOCTOR") {
      return <Navigate to="/doctor" replace />;
    }

    return <Navigate to="/login" replace />;
  }

  // Today's Appointments
  const today = new Date();

  const todaysAppointments = appointments.filter((appointment) => {
    const appointmentDate = new Date(appointment.appointmentDate);

    return (
      appointmentDate.getDate() === today.getDate() &&
      appointmentDate.getMonth() === today.getMonth() &&
      appointmentDate.getFullYear() === today.getFullYear()
    );
  });

  // Sidebar Active Links
  const isActive = (path) => location.pathname === path;

  const isPatientsActive =
    location.pathname.startsWith("/receptionist/patients") ||
    location.pathname === "/receptionist/add-patient" ||
    location.pathname.startsWith("/receptionist/edit-patient");

  const isAppointmentsActive =
    location.pathname.startsWith("/receptionist/appointments") ||
    location.pathname === "/receptionist/add-appointment" ||
    location.pathname.startsWith("/receptionist/edit-appointment");

  // Logout
  function handleLogout() {
    localStorage.removeItem("token");
    localStorage.removeItem("user");

    toast.success("Logged out successfully");
    navigate("/login", { replace: true });
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full">

      {/* Sidebar */}
      <div className="flex w-64 flex-col border-r bg-white p-5">

        <h1 className="mb-10 text-xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Hospital MS
        </h1>

        {/* Dashboard */}
        <Link
          to="/receptionist"
          className={`mb-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg p-3 ${
            isActive("/receptionist")
              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
              : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
          }`}
        >
          <LayoutDashboard size={20} />
          Dashboard
        </Link>

        {/* Patients */}
        <Link
          to="/receptionist/patients"
          className={`mb-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg p-3 ${
            isPatientsActive
              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
              : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
          }`}
        >
          <Users size={20} />
          Patients
        </Link>

        {/* Appointments */}
        <Link
          to="/receptionist/appointments"
          className={`mb-3 flex items-center gap-3 rounded-lg p-3 ${
            isAppointmentsActive
              ? "bg-blue-600 text-white"
              : "text-gray-600 hover:bg-gray-100"
          }`}
        >
          <CalendarDays size={20} />
          Appointments
        </Link>

        {/* Logout */}
        <div className="mt-auto border-t pt-5">
          <button
            onClick={handleLogout}
            className="flex w-full items-center gap-3 rounded-lg p-3 text-red-600 hover:bg-red-50"
          >
            <LogOut size={20} />
            Logout
          </button>
        </div>

      </div>

      {/* Main Content */}
      <div className="flex-1 bg-gray-50 p-8">

        <Routes>

          {/* Dashboard */}
          <Route
            path="/"
            element={
              <div>
                <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
                  Receptionist Dashboard
                </h1>

                <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
                  Welcome, {user.firstName} {user.lastName}.
                </p>

                {/* Dashboard Cards */}
                <div className="mt-8 grid grid-cols-1 gap-6 md:grid-cols-3">

                  {/* Total Patients */}
                  <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
                          Total Patients
                        </p>

                        <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900">
                          {isLoading ? "..." : patients.length}
                        </h2>
                      </div>

                      <div className="rounded-lg bg-blue-100 p-3 text-blue-600">
                        <Users size={26} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Total Appointments */}
                  <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
                          Total Appointments
                        </p>

                        <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900">
                          {isLoading ? "..." : appointments.length}
                        </h2>
                      </div>

                      <div className="rounded-lg bg-green-100 p-3 text-green-600">
                        <CalendarDays size={26} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                  {/* Today's Appointments */}
                  <div className="rounded-xl bg-white p-6 shadow-sm">
                    <div className="flex items-center justify-between">
                      <div>
                        <p className="text-sm font-medium text-gray-500">
                          Today's Appointments
                        </p>

                        <h2 className="mt-2 text-3xl font-bold text-gray-900">
                          {isLoading ? "..." : todaysAppointments.length}
                        </h2>
                      </div>

                      <div className="rounded-lg bg-purple-100 p-3 text-purple-600">
                        <Clock size={26} />
                      </div>
                    </div>
                  </div>

                </div>
              </div>
            }
          />

          {/* Patient Routes */}
          <Route
            path="/patients"
            element={<ReceptionistPatientsPage />}
          />

          <Route
            path="/add-patient"
            element={<ReceptionistAddPatientPage />}
          />

          <Route
            path="/edit-patient/:patientId"
            element={<ReceptionistEditPatientPage />}
          />

          {/* Appointment Routes */}
          <Route
            path="/appointments"
            element={<ReceptionistAppointmentsPage />}
          />

          <Route
            path="/add-appointment"
            element={<ReceptionistAddAppointmentPage />}
          />

          <Route
            path="/edit-appointment/:appointmentId"
            element={<ReceptionistEditAppointmentPage />}
          />

        </Routes>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import { useEffect, useState } from "react";
import { useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { Plus, Pencil } from "lucide-react";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function ReceptionistPatientsPage() {
  const navigate = useNavigate();

  const [patients, setPatients] = useState([]);
  const [isLoading, setIsLoading] = useState(true);
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  useEffect(() => {
    const token = localStorage.getItem("token");

    api.get("/patients", {
      headers: {
        Authorization: `Bearer ${token}`,
      },
    })
      .then((res) => {
        setPatients(res.data.patients || []);
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.log(err);

        setError(
          err.response?.data?.message ||
          "Failed to load patients"
        );
      })
      .finally(() => {
        setIsLoading(false);
      });
  }, []);

  return (
    <div>
      {/* Page Header */}
      <div className="flex flex-col justify-between gap-4 sm:flex-row sm:items-center">
        <div>
          <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
            Patients
          </h1>

          <p className="mt-2 text-gray-600">
            Manage patient information and records.
          </p>
        </div>

        <button
          onClick={() => navigate("/receptionist/add-patient")}
          className="flex items-center justify-center gap-2 rounded-lg bg-blue-600 px-5 py-3 font-medium text-white hover:bg-blue-700"
        >
          <Plus size={20} />
          Add Patient
        </button>
      </div>

      {/* Patients Table */}
      <div className="mt-6 overflow-x-auto rounded-lg bg-white shadow-sm">
        {isLoading ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">
            Loading patients...
          </p>
        ) : error ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-red-600">
            {error}
          </p>
        ) : patients.length === 0 ? (
          <p className="p-6 text-gray-500">
            No patients found.
          </p>
        ) : (
          <table className="w-full text-left text-sm">
            <thead className="bg-gray-100 text-gray-700">
              <tr>
                <th className="p-4">Patient Name</th>
                <th className="p-4">Date of Birth</th>
                <th className="p-4">Phone</th>
                <th className="p-4">Email</th>
                <th className="p-4">Status</th>
                <th className="p-4">Action</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>

            <tbody>
              {patients.map((patient) => (
                <tr
                  key={patient.id}
                  className="border-t hover:bg-gray-50"
                >
                  <td className="p-4 font-medium">
                    {patient.firstName} {patient.lastName}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {patient.dateOfBirth
                      ? new Date(
                          patient.dateOfBirth
                        ).toLocaleDateString()
                      : "-"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {patient.phone || "-"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    {patient.email || "-"}
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    <span
                      className={`rounded-full px-3 py-1 text-xs font-medium ${
                        patient.isActive
                          ? "bg-green-100 text-green-700"
                          : "bg-gray-100 text-gray-600"
                      }`}
                    >
                      {patient.isActive ? "Active" : "Inactive"}
                    </span>
                  </td>

                  <td className="p-4">
                    <button
                      onClick={() =>
                        navigate(
                          `/receptionist/edit-patient/${patient.id}`
                        )
                      }
                      className="flex items-center gap-2 rounded-lg border border-blue-200 px-3 py-2 text-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-50"
                    >
                      <Pencil size={16} />
                      Edit
                    </button>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        )}
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}


========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\frontend\src\pages\registerPage.jsx ==========


import { Link, useNavigate } from "react-router-dom";
import { useState } from "react";
import api from "../lib/api";

export default function RegisterPage() {
  const [email, setEmail] = useState("");
  const [firstName, setFirstName] = useState("");
  const [lastName, setLastName] = useState("");
  const [password, setPassword] = useState("");
  const [confirmPassword, setConfirmPassword] = useState("");
  const [error, setError] = useState("");

  const navigate = useNavigate();

  function handleRegister() {
    setError("");

    if (password !== confirmPassword) {
      setError("Passwords do not match");
      return;
    }

    api.post("/auth/register", {
      email: email,
      firstName: firstName,
      lastName: lastName,
      password: password,
    })
      .then(() => {
        navigate("/login");
      })
      .catch((err) => {
        console.log(err);
        setError(
          err.response?.data?.message || "Registration failed"
        );
      });
  }

  return (
    <div className="flex min-h-screen w-full items-center justify-center bg-gray-50 px-4">
      <div className="flex w-full max-w-md flex-col items-center rounded-xl bg-white p-8 shadow-lg">

        <h1 className="text-2xl font-bold text-blue-900">
          Hospital Management System
        </h1>

        <h2 className="mt-4 text-xl font-semibold text-gray-700">
          Create Account
        </h2>

        <p className="mt-1 text-sm text-gray-500">
          Register as a receptionist
        </p>

        <label className="mt-5 w-full font-medium text-gray-700">
          Email
        </label>

        <input
          value={email}
          onChange={(e) => setEmail(e.target.value)}
          type="email"
          className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          placeholder="user@gmail.com"
        />

        <div className="mt-4 flex w-full gap-3">
          <div className="flex w-1/2 flex-col">
            <label className="font-medium text-gray-700">
              First Name
            </label>
            <input
              value={firstName}
              onChange={(e) => setFirstName(e.target.value)}
              type="text"
              className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              placeholder="First name"
            />
          </div>

          <div className="flex w-1/2 flex-col">
            <label className="font-medium text-gray-700">
              Last Name
            </label>
            <input
              value={lastName}
              onChange={(e) => setLastName(e.target.value)}
              type="text"
              className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
              placeholder="Last name"
            />
          </div>
        </div>

        <label className="mt-4 w-full font-medium text-gray-700">
          Password
        </label>

        <input
          value={password}
          onChange={(e) => setPassword(e.target.value)}
          type="password"
          className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          placeholder="Minimum 8 characters"
        />

        <label className="mt-4 w-full font-medium text-gray-700">
          Confirm Password
        </label>

        <input
          value={confirmPassword}
          onChange={(e) => setConfirmPassword(e.target.value)}
          type="password"
          className="mt-2 h-11 w-full rounded-lg border px-3 outline-none focus:border-blue-500"
          placeholder="Confirm password"
        />

        {error && (
          <p className="mt-3 w-full text-sm text-red-600">
            {error}
          </p>
        )}

        <button
          onClick={handleRegister}
          className="mt-6 h-11 w-full rounded-lg bg-blue-600 font-semibold text-white hover:bg-blue-700"
        >
          Register
        </button>

        <p className="mt-4 w-full text-right text-sm text-gray-600">
          Already have an account?{" "}
          <Link to="/login" className="font-semibold text-blue-600">
            Login here
          </Link>
        </p>

      </div>
    </div>
  );
}

import "dotenv/config";

import cors from "cors";

import authRouter from "./routes/authRoutes.js";
import patientRouter from "./routes/patientRoutes.js";
import doctorRouter from "./routes/doctorRoutes.js";
import appointmentRouter from "./routes/appointmentRoutes.js";

app.use(express.json());

app.get("/", (req, res) => {
  res.status(200).json({
    message: "Hospital Management System API is running",
  });
});

app.use("/api/auth", authRouter);
app.use("/api/patients", patientRouter);
app.use("/api/doctors", doctorRouter);
app.use("/api/appointments", appointmentRouter);
app.use("/api/medical-records", medicalRecordRouter);
app.use("/api/invoices", invoiceRouter);

const PORT = process.env.PORT || 5000;

app.listen(PORT, () => {
  console.log(`Server is running on port ${PORT}`);

import express from "express";

import {
  createAppointment,
  getAllAppointments,
  getAppointmentById,
  updateAppointment,
  cancelAppointment,
} from "../controllers/appointmentController.js";

import {
  verifyToken,
  checkRole,
} from "../middleware/authMiddleware.js";

const appointmentRouter = express.Router();

// Create Appointment
appointmentRouter.post(
  "/",
  verifyToken,
  checkRole("ADMIN", "RECEPTIONIST"),
  createAppointment
);

// Get All Appointments
appointmentRouter.get(
  "/",
  verifyToken,
  checkRole("ADMIN", "DOCTOR", "RECEPTIONIST"),
  getAllAppointments
);

// Get Appointment By ID
appointmentRouter.get(
  "/:appointmentId",
  verifyToken,
  checkRole("ADMIN", "DOCTOR", "RECEPTIONIST"),
  getAppointmentById
);

// Update Appointment
appointmentRouter.put(
  "/:appointmentId",
  verifyToken,
  checkRole("ADMIN", "RECEPTIONIST"),
  updateAppointment
);

// Cancel Appointment
appointmentRouter.patch(
  "/:appointmentId/cancel",
  verifyToken,
  checkRole("ADMIN", "RECEPTIONIST"),
  cancelAppointment
);

export default appointmentRouter;

========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\backend\src\middleware\authMiddleware.js ==========


import jwt from "jsonwebtoken";

// VERIFY JWT TOKEN
export function verifyToken(req, res, next) {
  try {
    const authHeader = req.headers.authorization;

    const token = authHeader?.startsWith("Bearer ")
      ? authHeader.split(" ")[1]
      : null;

    if (!token) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        message: "Access denied. No token provided",
      });
    }

    if (!process.env.JWT_SECRET) {
      return res.status(500).json({
        message: "JWT configuration error",
      });
    }

    const decoded = jwt.verify(token, process.env.JWT_SECRET);

    req.user = decoded;

    next();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Token verification error:", error.message);

    return res.status(401).json({
      message: "Invalid or expired token",
    });
  }
}

// CHECK USER ROLE
export function checkRole(...allowedRoles) {
  return (req, res, next) => {
    if (!req.user) {
      return res.status(401).json({
        message: "Authentication required",
      });
    }

    if (!allowedRoles.includes(req.user.role)) {
      return res.status(403).json({
        message: "Access denied. You don't have permission",
      });
    }

    next();
  };
}

const pool = new Pool({
  connectionString: process.env.DATABASE_URL,
});

const prisma = new PrismaClient({
  adapter,
});

export default prisma;

========== C:\Users\hp pc\Desktop\hospital-system\backend\src\controllers\appointmentController.js ==========


import prisma from "../lib/prisma.js";

// Create Appointment
export async function createAppointment(req, res) {
  try {
    const {
      patientId,
      doctorId,
      appointmentDate,
      reason,
    } = req.body;

    if (!patientId || !doctorId || !appointmentDate) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Patient, doctor and appointment date are required",
      });
    }

    const date = new Date(appointmentDate);

    if (isNaN(date.getTime())) {
      return res.status(400).json({
        message: "Invalid appointment date",
      });
    }

    const patient = await prisma.patient.findFirst({
      where: {
        id: patientId,
        isActive: true,
      },
    });

    if (!patient) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Active patient not found",
      });
    }

    const doctor = await prisma.doctor.findFirst({
      where: {
        id: doctorId,
        user: {
          isActive: true,
        },
      },
    });

    if (!doctor) {
      return res.status(404).json({
        message: "Active doctor not found",
      });
    }

    const appointment = await prisma.appointment.create({
      data: {
        patientId,
        doctorId,
        appointmentDate: date,
        reason,
      },
    });

    return res.status(201).json({
      message: "Appointment created successfully",
      appointment,
    });
  } catch (error) {
    console.error("Create appointment error:", error);

    return res.status(500).json({
      message: "Internal server error",
    });
  }
}

// Get All Appointments

      req.user.role === "DOCTOR"
